## Execution Protocol

- Keep scheduled tasks disabled while draining this checklist manually.
- Run 5-10 unchecked tasks concurrently as a worker pool; when one task hangs, keep moving with other unchecked tasks.
- Mark a line as `[x]` only after that task completes successfully, and update `status` from `todo` to `done`.
- If a task fails because of timeout, increase the manual execution timeout and retry before marking it blocked.
- If a task fails because of an Akshare/interface issue, fix the script or interface first, then retry the same unchecked task.
- If a task remains stuck after retries, leave it unchecked, set `status=blocked`, add a short `note=...`, and continue.
